def count_vowels(s):
    count = 0
    for char in s:
        if char in 'aeiouAEIOU':
            count += 1
    return count

def factorial(n):
    if n == 0:

def fibonacci(arg0):
    if arg0 == 0:
        return 0
    if arg0 == 1:
        return 1
    a, b = 0, 1
    for _ in range(2, arg0 + 1):
        a, b = b, a + b
    return b

def fibonacci(arg0):
    if arg0 == 0:
        return 0
    elif arg0 == 1:
        return 1
    else:
        a, b = 0, 1
        for _ in range(2, arg0 + 1):
            a, b = b, a + b
        return b

def word_count(s):
    word_counts = {}
    for word in s.split():
        if word in word_counts:
            word_counts[word] += 1
        else:
            word_counts[word] = 1
    return word_counts

    return Counter(s.split())

def word_count(s):
    words = s.split()
    result = {}
    for word in words:
        result[word] = result.get(word, 0) + 1
    return result

def word_count(arg0):
    if not arg0:
        return {}
    words = arg0.split()
    counts = {}
    for word in words:
        counts[word] = counts.get(word, 0) + 1
    return counts

def filter_evens(nums):
    result = []
    for num in nums:
        if num % 2 == 0:
            result.append(num)
    return result

def get_initials(name):
    name = name.strip()
    initials = ""
    for i in name:
        if i.isalpha():
            initials += i
    return initials

    return True

def fizzbuzz_list(n):
    result = []
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        if i % 3 == 0 and i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('FizzBuzz')
        elif i % 3 == 0:
            result.append('Fizz')
        elif i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('Buzz')
        else:
            result.append(str(i))
    return result

def fizzbuzz_list(n):
    result = []
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        if i % 3 == 0 and i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('FizzBuzz')
        elif i % 3 == 0:
            result.append('Fizz')
        elif i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('Buzz')
        else:
            result.append(str(i))
    return result

def fizzbuzz_list(n):
    result = []
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        if i % 3 == 0 and i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('FizzBuzz')
        elif i % 3 == 0:
            result.append('Fizz')
        elif i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('Buzz')
        else:
            result.append(str(i))
    return result

def fizzbuzz_list(arg0):
    """Return a list of strings for 1..n where multiples of 3 are 'Fizz', multiples of 5 are 'Buzz', multiples of both are 'FizzBuzz', else the number as a string."""
    result = []
    for i in range(1, arg0 + 1):
        if i % 3 == 0 and i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('FizzBuzz')
        elif i % 3 == 0:
            result.append('Fizz')
        elif i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('Buzz')
        else:
            result.append(str(i))
    return result

def fizzbuzz_list(n):
    result = []
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        if i % 3 == 0 and i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('FizzBuzz')
        elif i % 3 == 0:
            result.append('Fizz')
        elif i % 5 == 0:
            result.append('Buzz')
        else:
            result.append(str(i))
    return result
